===== FILE: server.js =====
const express = require('express');
const fs = require('fs');
const path = require('path');
const app = express();
const PORT = process.env.PORT || 3000;
const HOST = process.env.HOST || '0.0.0.0';
const LOG_PATH = '/tmp/fr-ven-server.log';
const log = (msg) => {
  const line = `[${new Date().toISOString()}] ${msg}\n`;
  try { fs.appendFileSync(LOG_PATH, line); } catch {}
  console.log(msg);
};

log('Iniciando servidor...');

const TEMPLATE_DIR = path.join(__dirname, 'templates');
const PUBLIC_DIR = path.join(__dirname, 'public');
const DATA_PATH = path.join(__dirname, 'data', 'sample.json');
const DATA_DIR = path.join(__dirname, 'data');
const SUBMISSIONS_PATH = path.join(DATA_DIR, 'submissions.json');

const formats = {
  '35': { name: 'FR-VEN-35 Aviso de Privacidad', file: 'format-35.html' },
  '21': { name: 'FR-VEN-21 Consulta de Listas de Personas Bloqueadas', file: 'format-21.html' },
  '26': { name: 'FR-VEN-26 Origen de los Recursos', file: 'format-26.html' },
  '19': { name: 'FR-VEN-19 Beneficiario Controlador PF', file: 'format-19.html' },
  '10': { name: 'FR-VEN-10 Identificación del Cliente (Extranjera PF)', file: 'format-10.html' }
};

app.use(express.json());
app.use(express.urlencoded({ extended: true }));
app.use('/assets', express.static(path.join(PUBLIC_DIR, 'assets')));

process.on('uncaughtException', (err) => {
  log(`Uncaught exception: ${err && err.stack ? err.stack : err}`);
});
process.on('unhandledRejection', (err) => {
  log(`Unhandled rejection: ${err && err.stack ? err.stack : err}`);
});

function ensureDataFiles() {
  if (!fs.existsSync(DATA_DIR)) fs.mkdirSync(DATA_DIR, { recursive: true });
  if (!fs.existsSync(SUBMISSIONS_PATH)) fs.writeFileSync(SUBMISSIONS_PATH, '[]', 'utf-8');
  if (!fs.existsSync(DATA_PATH)) fs.writeFileSync(DATA_PATH, '{}', 'utf-8');
}

function readJson(filePath, fallback) {
  try {
    if (!fs.existsSync(filePath)) return fallback;
    return JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(filePath, 'utf-8'));
  } catch (err) {
    log(`Error leyendo ${filePath}: ${err.message}`);
    return fallback;
  }
}

function writeJson(filePath, data) {
  const tmpPath = `${filePath}.tmp`;
  fs.writeFileSync(tmpPath, JSON.stringify(data, null, 2), 'utf-8');
  fs.renameSync(tmpPath, filePath);
}

function persistSubmission(formatId, formatName, payload) {
  const current = readJson(SUBMISSIONS_PATH, []);
  const createdAt = new Date().toISOString();
  const record = {
    id: `${Date.now()}-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 10)}`,
    createdAt,
    formatId,
    formatName,
    payload
  };
  current.push(record);
  writeJson(SUBMISSIONS_PATH, current);
  // Keep latest payload for preview routes
  writeJson(DATA_PATH, payload);
  return record;
}

ensureDataFiles();

function renderTemplate(templateName, data, options = {}) {
  const filePath = path.join(TEMPLATE_DIR, templateName);
  let html = fs.readFileSync(filePath, 'utf-8');

  const watermarkUrl = options.baseUrl
    ? `${options.baseUrl}/assets/watermark.png`
    : '/assets/watermark.png';

  const controls = options.showControls
    ? `<button class="btn" onclick="window.location.href='${options.downloadUrl}'">Descargar PDF</button>`
    : '';

  html = html.replace(/{{watermarkUrl}}/g, watermarkUrl);
  html = html.replace(/{{controls}}/g, controls);
  html = html.replace(/{{bodyClass}}/g, options.bodyClass || 'interactive');

  // Simple {{key}} replacement using provided data
  Object.entries(data || {}).forEach(([key, value]) => {
    const re = new RegExp(`{{\\s*${key}\\s*}}`, 'g');
    html = html.replace(re, String(value));
  });

  return html;
}

app.get('/', (req, res) => {
  res.send(`<!doctype html>

app.get('/legacy/formatos', (req, res) => {
  const items = Object.entries(formats)
    .map(([id, f]) => `<li><a href="/format/${id}">${f.name}</a></li>`)
    .join('');

  res.send(`<!doctype html>
  <html lang="es"><head><meta charset="utf-8" />
  <title>Formatos FR-VEN</title>
  <style>
    body{font-family: Arial, sans-serif; padding:24px;}
    li{margin:8px 0;}
  </style></head><body>
    <h1>Formatos disponibles</h1>
    <ul>${items}</ul>
    <p><a href="/">Volver al panel</a></p>
  </body></html>`);
});

app.get('/generador-faes', (req, res) => {
  res.send(`<!doctype html><html lang="es"><head><meta charset="utf-8"><title>Generador FAES</title></head><body style="font-family:Arial;padding:24px"><h1>Generador FAES</h1><p>Sección en construcción.</p><p><a href="/">Volver al panel</a></p></body></html>`);
});

app.get('/plds', (req, res) => {
  res.send(`<!doctype html><html lang="es"><head><meta charset="utf-8"><title>PLDS</title></head><body style="font-family:Arial;padding:24px"><h1>PLDS</h1><p>Sección en construcción.</p><p><a href="/">Volver al panel</a></p></body></html>`);
});

app.get('/generador-roi', (req, res) => {
  res.sendFile(path.join(PUBLIC_DIR, 'generador-roi.html'));
});

app.get('/form', (req, res) => {
  res.sendFile(path.join(PUBLIC_DIR, 'form.html'));
});

app.get('/health', (req, res) => {
  res.status(200).json({ ok: true, ts: new Date().toISOString() });
});

app.get('/submissions', (req, res) => {
  const limitRaw = Number(req.query.limit || 50);
  const limit = Number.isFinite(limitRaw) ? Math.max(1, Math.min(limitRaw, 500)) : 50;
  const all = readJson(SUBMISSIONS_PATH, []);
  const items = all.slice(-limit).reverse();
  res.json({ total: all.length, items });
});

app.get('/format/:id', (req, res) => {
  const id = req.params.id;
  const format = formats[id];
  if (!format) return res.status(404).send('Formato no encontrado');

  const baseUrl = `${req.protocol}://${req.get('host')}`;
  const data = fs.existsSync(DATA_PATH) ? JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(DATA_PATH, 'utf-8')) : {};
  const html = renderTemplate(format.file, data, {
    baseUrl,
    showControls: !req.query.print,
    downloadUrl: `/format/${id}/pdf`,
    bodyClass: 'interactive'
  });
  res.send(html);
});

app.get('/format/:id/pdf', async (req, res) => {
  const id = req.params.id;
  const format = formats[id];
  if (!format) return res.status(404).send('Formato no encontrado');

  const puppeteer = require('puppeteer');
  const baseUrl = `${req.protocol}://${req.get('host')}`;
  const data = fs.existsSync(DATA_PATH) ? JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(DATA_PATH, 'utf-8')) : {};
  const html = renderTemplate(format.file, data, {
    baseUrl,
    showControls: false,
    downloadUrl: `/format/${id}/pdf`,
    bodyClass: 'print'
  });

  const browser = await puppeteer.launch({ headless: 'new' });
  try {
    const page = await browser.newPage();
    await page.setContent(html, { waitUntil: 'networkidle0' });
    const pdf = await page.pdf({
      format: 'Letter',
      printBackground: true,
      margin: { top: '0in', right: '0in', bottom: '0in', left: '0in' }
    });

    res.set({
      'Content-Type': 'application/pdf',
      'Content-Disposition': `attachment; filename=${format.file.replace('.html', '')}.pdf`
    });
    res.send(pdf);
  } finally {
    await browser.close();
  }
});

app.post('/format/:id/pdf', async (req, res) => {
  const id = req.params.id;
  const format = formats[id];
  if (!format) return res.status(404).send('Formato no encontrado');

  const data = { ...(req.body || {}) };
  if (id === '10' || id === '19' || id === '21' || id === '35') {
    // Replace empty text fields with N/A
    Object.keys(data).forEach((k) => {
      const v = data[k];
      if (typeof v === 'string' && v.trim() === '') data[k] = 'N/A';
    });
  }

  const saved = persistSubmission(id, format.name, data);
  log(`Submission guardado: ${saved.id} formato=${id}`);

  const puppeteer = require('puppeteer');
  const baseUrl = `${req.protocol}://${req.get('host')}`;
  const html = renderTemplate(format.file, data, {
    baseUrl,
    showControls: false,
    downloadUrl: `/format/${id}/pdf`,
    bodyClass: 'print'
  });

  const browser = await puppeteer.launch({ headless: 'new' });
  try {
    const page = await browser.newPage();
    await page.setContent(html, { waitUntil: 'networkidle0' });
    const pdfOpts = {
      format: 'letter',
      printBackground: true,
      margin: { top: '0in', right: '0in', bottom: '0.6in', left: '0in' }
    };
    // FR-VEN-19 footer is rendered in HTML to match FR-VEN-10 layout
    const pdf = await page.pdf(pdfOpts);

    res.set({
      'Content-Type': 'application/pdf',
      'Content-Disposition': `attachment; filename=${format.file.replace('.html', '')}.pdf`
    });
    res.send(pdf);
  } finally {
    await browser.close();
  }
});

const server = app.listen(PORT, HOST, () => {
  log(`Servidor listo en http://${HOST}:${PORT}`);
});
server.on('error', (err) => {
  log(`Error al iniciar servidor: ${err && err.stack ? err.stack : err}`);
});

<script>
  const form = document.querySelector('.wrap');

  function syncByName(source) {
    if (!source.name) return;
    const peers = form.querySelectorAll(`[name="${CSS.escape(source.name)}"]`);
    peers.forEach((peer) => {
      if (peer === source) return;
      if (source.type === 'radio') {
        peer.checked = source.checked && peer.value === source.value;
      } else if (source.type === 'checkbox') {
        peer.checked = source.checked;
      } else {
        peer.value = source.value;
      }
    });
  }

  function syncAliases(source) {
    // FR-VEN-10 -> FR-VEN-21
    if (source.name === 'spanTipoIdentificacion') {
      form.querySelectorAll('input[name="v21_tipo_id"]').forEach((el) => {
        if (el !== source) el.value = source.value;
      });
    }
  }

  form.querySelectorAll('input, select').forEach((el) => {
    el.addEventListener('input', () => { syncByName(el); syncAliases(el); });
    el.addEventListener('change', () => { syncByName(el); syncAliases(el); });
  });

  function getData() {
    const data = {};
    form.querySelectorAll('input, select').forEach((el) => {
      if (el.type === 'checkbox') {
        if (el.checked) data[el.name] = 'checked';
      } else if (el.type === 'radio') {
        if (el.checked) data[el.name] = el.value;
      } else {
        const value = (el.value || '').trim();
        // Evita que inputs duplicados vacíos sobreescriban valores capturados.
        if (!(el.name in data) || !data[el.name]) {
          data[el.name] = value;
        } else if (value) {
          data[el.name] = value;
        }
      }
    });

    // Firma del cliente: usar nombre completo como fuente principal.
    data.cbNombre1 = (data.spanNombreCompleto || data.cbNombre || data.cbNombre1 || '').trim();

    // shared mappings
    data.txtLoteES = data.cbUnidadES || '';
    data.txtDesarrolloES = data.cbDesarrolloES || '';
    data.txtCiudadES = data.txtCiudadES || '';
    data.desarrolladoraText = data.desarrolladoraText || '';

    // EN mirrored from ES for FR-VEN-10 and 19
    data.txtLoteEN = data.txtLoteES || '';
    data.txtDesarrolloEN = data.txtDesarrolloES || '';
    data.txtCiudadEN = data.txtCiudadES || '';

    data.cbLugarEN = data.cbLugarES || '';
    data.cbDiaEN = data.cbDiaES || '';
    data.cbMesEN = data.cbMesES || '';
    data.cbAnioEN = data.cbAnioES || '';
    data.cbEmpresaEN = data.desarrolladoraText || '';
    data.cbEmpresaES = data.desarrolladoraText || '';
    data.cbEmpresaDomicilioEN = data.cbEmpresaDomicilioES || '';
    data.cbDesarrolloEN = data.cbDesarrolloES || '';
    data.cbUnidadEN = data.cbUnidadES || '';

    if (data.pepCliente === 'yes') data.pepClienteYes = 'checked';
    if (data.pepCliente === 'no') data.pepClienteNo = 'checked';
    if (data.pepFamiliar === 'yes') data.pepFamiliarYes = 'checked';
    if (data.pepFamiliar === 'no') data.pepFamiliarNo = 'checked';

    if (data.cbExiste === 'yes') data.cbExisteyes = 'checked';
    if (data.cbExiste === 'no') data.cbExisteno = 'checked';

    // FR-VEN-26 shared mappings
    data.orLugarES = data.cbLugarES || '';
    data.orDiaES = data.cbDiaES || '';
    data.orMesES = data.cbMesES || '';
    data.orAnioES = data.cbAnioES || '';
    data.orLugarEN = data.orLugarES || '';
    data.orDiaEN = data.orDiaES || '';
    data.orMesEN = data.orMesES || '';
    data.orAnioEN = data.orAnioES || '';

    data.orEmpresaES = data.desarrolladoraText || '';
    data.orEmpresaEN = data.desarrolladoraText || '';
    data.orEmpresaDirES = data.cbEmpresaDomicilioES || '';
    data.orEmpresaDirEN = data.cbEmpresaDomicilioES || '';

    data.orDesarrolloES = data.cbDesarrolloES || '';
    data.orDesarrolloEN = data.cbDesarrolloES || '';
    data.orUnidadES = data.cbUnidadES || '';
    data.orUnidadEN = data.cbUnidadES || '';
    data.orDesarrolloBodyES = data.cbDesarrolloES || '';
    data.orDesarrolloBodyEN = data.cbDesarrolloES || '';
    data.orUnidadBodyES = data.cbUnidadES || '';
    data.orUnidadBodyEN = data.cbUnidadES || '';
    data.orCiudadES = data.txtCiudadES || '';
    data.orCiudadEN = data.txtCiudadES || '';

    if (data.orTipo === 'propios') data.orPropios = 'checked';
    if (data.orTipo === 'tercero') data.orTercero = 'checked';

    // FR-VEN-35 mappings
    data.spanFecha = (data.spanFecha && data.spanFecha.trim()) ? data.spanFecha : [data.cbDiaES || '', data.cbMesES || '', data.cbAnioES || ''].filter(Boolean).join(' / ');
    data.solicitarSi = '';
    data.solicitarNo = '';
    data.solicitarSiEN = '';
    data.solicitarNoEN = '';
    data.enviarSi = '';
    data.enviarNo = '';
    data.enviarSiEN = '';
    data.enviarNoEN = '';

    if (data.pvSolicitar === 'si') {
      data.solicitarSi = 'checked';
      data.solicitarSiEN = 'checked';
    } else if (data.pvSolicitar === 'no') {
      data.solicitarNo = 'checked';
      data.solicitarNoEN = 'checked';
    }
    if (data.pvEnviar === 'si') {
      data.enviarSi = 'checked';
      data.enviarSiEN = 'checked';
    } else if (data.pvEnviar === 'no') {
      data.enviarNo = 'checked';
      data.enviarNoEN = 'checked';
    }

    // FR-VEN-21 mappings
    data.lugar_emision = data.cbLugarES || '';
    data.dia = data.cbDiaES || '';
    data.mes = data.cbMesES || '';
    data.anio = data.cbAnioES || '';
    data.nombre_desarrollo = data.cbDesarrolloES || '';
    data.numero_unidad = data.cbUnidadES || '';
    data.empresa_grupo = data.desarrolladoraText || '';
    data.nombre_desarrollo_2 = data.cbDesarrolloES || '';
    data.numero_unidad_2 = data.cbUnidadES || '';
    data.ciudad = data.txtCiudadES || '';
    data.cliente_nombre = data.spanNombreCompleto || '';
    data.cliente_identificacion = data.v21_tipo_id || data.spanTipoIdentificacion || '';
    // Regla solicitada: en FR-VEN-21 "Nacionalidad" toma "Lugar de nacimiento" de FR-VEN-10.
    data.cliente_nacionalidad = data.spanLugarNacimiento || data.spanNacionalidad || '';
    data.cliente_domicilio = data.spanDomicilio || '';
    data.cliente_rfc = data.spanIdentificacionFiscal || '';
    data.cliente_curp = data.v21_curp || '';
    data.vendedor_nombre = data.v21_vendedor || data.cbNombre1 || '';

    return data;
  }

  async function download(format) {
    const payload = getData();
    const res = await fetch(`/format/${format}/pdf`, {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify(payload)
    });
    const blob = await res.blob();
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const a = document.createElement('a');
    a.href = url;
    const rawName = (payload.spanNombreCompleto || '').trim();
    const safeName = (rawName || 'SIN_NOMBRE')
      .replace(/[\\/:*?"<>|]/g, ' ')
      .replace(/\s+/g, ' ')
      .trim();
    a.download = `FR-VEN-${format} ${safeName}.pdf`;
    document.body.appendChild(a);
    a.click();
    a.remove();
    URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
  }

  document.getElementById('download21').addEventListener('click', () => download('21'));
  document.getElementById('download10').addEventListener('click', () => download('10'));
  document.getElementById('download19').addEventListener('click', () => download('19'));
  document.getElementById('download26').addEventListener('click', () => download('26'));
  document.getElementById('download35').addEventListener('click', () => download('35'));
</script>
</body>
</html>

<script>
(function paginate() {
  const blocks = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('#blocks .block'));
  const pages = document.getElementById('pages');

  function createPage() {
    const page = document.createElement('div');
    page.className = 'page';
    const content = document.createElement('div');
    content.className = 'content';
    const footer = document.createElement('div');
    footer.className = 'footer';
    footer.innerHTML = `
      <div class="footer-left">FR-VEN-19 BC PF B</div>
      <div class="footer-center">version 01</div>
      <div class="footer-right">0</div>
    `;
    page.appendChild(content);
    page.appendChild(footer);
    pages.appendChild(page);
    return { page, content, footer };
  }

  let current = createPage();
  for (const block of blocks) {
    current.content.appendChild(block);
    if (current.content.scrollHeight > current.content.clientHeight) {
      current.content.removeChild(block);
      current = createPage();
      current.content.appendChild(block);
    }
  }

  // update page numbers
  const pageEls = pages.querySelectorAll('.page');
  pageEls.forEach((p, i) => {
    const num = p.querySelector('.footer-right');
    if (num) num.textContent = String(i + 1);
  });
})();
</script>

</body>
</html>

<script>
(function paginate() {
  const blocks = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('#blocks .block'));
  const pages = document.getElementById('pages');

  function createPage() {
    const page = document.createElement('div');
    page.className = 'page';
    const content = document.createElement('div');
    content.className = 'content';
    const footer = document.createElement('div');
    footer.className = 'footer';
    footer.innerHTML = `
      <div class="footer-left">FR-VEN-26 OR B</div>
      <div class="footer-center">VERSION 00</div>
      <div class="footer-right">0</div>
    `;
    page.appendChild(content);
    page.appendChild(footer);
    pages.appendChild(page);
    return { page, content, footer };
  }

  let current = createPage();
  for (const block of blocks) {
    current.content.appendChild(block);
    if (current.content.scrollHeight > current.content.clientHeight) {
      current.content.removeChild(block);
      current = createPage();
      current.content.appendChild(block);
    }
  }

  const pageEls = pages.querySelectorAll('.page');
  pageEls.forEach((p, i) => {
    const num = p.querySelector('.footer-right');
    if (num) num.textContent = String(i + 1);
  });
})();
</script>

</body>
</html>

Como consecuencia de sus Actividades Empresariales, GRUPO SIMCA recaba y/o tiene acceso a los datos personales que se señalan en el numeral 4 de este Aviso, propiedad de particulares que están interesados en adquirir, o bien, han iniciado el proceso de adquisición de una o varias Unidades (los "Datos Personales"). En el caso específico, GRUPO SIMCA ha recibido o recibirá los Datos Personales de la persona física o moral cuyo nombre/razón social y firma aparecen al calce del presente Aviso (el "Titular"), con quien GRUPO SIMCA ha iniciado o iniciará los trámites para la adquisición de una Unidad (la "Operación").</p>

        <p>i. Derecho de Acceso: Derecho a conocer qué Datos Personales tenemos del Titular, para qué finalidad y las condiciones del Tratamiento que GRUPO SIMCA les da.<br>ii. Derecho de Rectificación: Derecho a solicitar la corrección de sus Datos Personales en caso de que estén desactualizados, sean inexactos o estén incompletos.<br>iii. Derecho de Cancelación: Derecho a solicitar a GRUPO SIMCA la eliminación de los Datos Personales del Titular de sus registros o bases de datos cuando el Titular considere que los mismos no están siendo utilizados adecuadamente en términos de la Ley de Datos Personales y del presente Aviso.<br>iv. Derecho de Oposición: Derecho a oponerse al Tratamiento de los Datos Personales del Titular para ciertos fines específicos.<br>v. Derecho de Revocación: Derecho a revocar el Consentimiento (según se define en la fracción IV del artículo 2º de la Ley de Datos Personales) que por medio del presente Aviso otorga a GRUPO SIMCA para el Tratamiento de sus Datos Personales para las finalidades señaladas en los numerales 4.2.1 y 4.2.2 de este Aviso. No obstante ello, el Titular reconoce y acepta que, a pesar de la voluntad de GRUPO SIMCA de garantizar el acceso a este derecho, es posible que no en todos los casos GRUPO SIMCA podrá atender su solicitud o concluir el Tratamiento de forma inmediata. Además, el Titular reconoce y acepta que la revocación de su Consentimiento podría imposibilitar a GRUPO SIMCA para cerrar la Operación, o bien, continuar prestándole al Titular cualquier otro servicio relacionado con la misma e, incluso, en algunos casos, la terminación de la relación jurídica o comercial con GRUPO SIMCA.</p>

        <p>i. Right of Access: Right to know what Personal Data of the Holder is in possession of GRUPO SIMCA, what is the purpose of requiring such information and the terms and conditions of the Processing thereof.<br>ii. Right of Correction: Right to request the correction of the Personal Data of the Holder in case they are outdated, inaccurate or incomplete.<br>iii. Right of Cancellation: Right to request from GRUPO SIMCA the deletion of the Personal Data of the Holder from its records or databases if the Holder considers that they are not being used properly in accordance with the provisions of the Law on Personal Data and those of this Notice.<br>iv. Right of Opposition: Right to object to the Processing of the Personal Data of the Holder for certain specific purposes.<br>v. Right of Revocation: Right to revoke the Consent (as defined in section IV of article 2 of the Law on Personal Data) herein granted by GRUPO SIMCA for the Treatment of the Personal Data of the Holder for the purposes indicated in the paragraphs 4.2.1 and 4.2.2 hereof. Notwithstanding the foregoing, the Holder acknowledges and accepts that, despite GRUPO SIMCA's willingness to guarantee access to this right, it is possible that not in all cases GRUPO SIMCA shall be able to attend the Holder's request or terminate the Processing immediately. In addition, the Holder acknowledges and accepts that the revocation of its Consent could make it impossible for GRUPO SIMCA to close the Transaction, or continue to provide the Holder with any other service related thereto and, even in some cases, may cause the termination of the legal or commercial relationship with GRUPO SIMCA.</p>

  <script>
    (function paginate() {
      const sourceBlocks = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('#blocks .block'));
      const pages = document.getElementById('pages');
      const OVERFLOW_EPS = 1;

      function overflows(contentEl) {
        return contentEl.scrollHeight > contentEl.clientHeight + OVERFLOW_EPS;
      }

      function isMeaningful(block) {
        if (!block) return false;
        const text = (block.textContent || '').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
        if (text.length > 0) return true;
        return Boolean(block.querySelector('table, img, .signature, .field-line'));
      }

      function splitTwoColumnBlock(block) {
        if (!block.classList.contains('two-cols')) return [block];
        const cols = Array.from(block.children || []);
        if (cols.length !== 2) return [block];

        function splitParagraphNode(node) {
          const tag = (node.tagName || '').toUpperCase();
          if (tag !== 'P') return [node.cloneNode(true)];

          const html = node.innerHTML || '';
          const hasBreaks = /<br\s*\/?>/i.test(html);
          if (hasBreaks) {
            const parts = html
              .split(/<br\s*\/?>/i)
              .map((s) => s.trim())
              .filter(Boolean);
            if (parts.length <= 1) return [node.cloneNode(true)];

            const out = [];
            const chunkSize = 3;
            for (let i = 0; i < parts.length; i += chunkSize) {
              const p = document.createElement('p');
              p.innerHTML = parts.slice(i, i + chunkSize).join('<br>');
              out.push(p);
            }
            return out;
          }

          const text = (node.textContent || '').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
          if (text.length < 420) return [node.cloneNode(true)];

          const sentences = text.split(/(?<=[\.\?\!;:])\s+/).filter(Boolean);
          if (sentences.length <= 1) return [node.cloneNode(true)];

          const out = [];
          let bucket = [];
          let size = 0;
          const maxChars = 360;
          for (const s of sentences) {
            if (size + s.length > maxChars && bucket.length) {
              const p = document.createElement('p');
              p.textContent = bucket.join(' ');
              out.push(p);
              bucket = [s];
              size = s.length;
            } else {
              bucket.push(s);
              size += s.length;
            }
          }
          if (bucket.length) {
            const p = document.createElement('p');
            p.textContent = bucket.join(' ');
            out.push(p);
          }
          return out.length ? out : [node.cloneNode(true)];
        }

        function flattenColumnItems(colEl) {
          const children = Array.from(colEl.children || []);
          const out = [];
          for (const child of children) out.push(...splitParagraphNode(child));
          return out;
        }

        const leftItems = flattenColumnItems(cols[0]);
        const rightItems = flattenColumnItems(cols[1]);
        if (leftItems.length <= 1 && rightItems.length <= 1) return [block];

        const count = Math.max(leftItems.length, rightItems.length);
        const chunks = [];
        for (let i = 0; i < count; i += 1) {
          const l = leftItems[i];
          const r = rightItems[i];
          if (!l && !r) continue;

          const chunk = block.cloneNode(false);
          const lWrap = document.createElement('div');
          const rWrap = document.createElement('div');
          if (l) lWrap.appendChild(l.cloneNode(true));
          if (r) rWrap.appendChild(r.cloneNode(true));
          chunk.appendChild(lWrap);
          chunk.appendChild(rWrap);
          chunks.push(chunk);
        }
        return chunks.length ? chunks : [block];
      }

      function expandBlock(block) {
        const parts = splitTwoColumnBlock(block);
        if (parts.length === 1) return [block.cloneNode(true)];
        return parts;
      }

      function createPage() {
        const page = document.createElement('section');
        page.className = 'page';
        const content = document.createElement('div');
        content.className = 'content';
        const footer = document.createElement('div');
        footer.className = 'footer';
        footer.innerHTML = '<div class="l">FR-VEN-35 AP B</div><div class="c">version 01</div><div class="r">0</div>';
        page.appendChild(content);
        page.appendChild(footer);
        pages.appendChild(page);
        return { page, content };
      }

      const queue = sourceBlocks
        .flatMap((b) => expandBlock(b))
        .filter((b) => isMeaningful(b));
      let current = createPage();

      while (queue.length) {
        const block = queue.shift();

        current.content.appendChild(block);
        if (!overflows(current.content)) continue;

        current.content.removeChild(block);
        current = createPage();
        current.content.appendChild(block);
      }

      const pageEls = Array.from(pages.querySelectorAll('.page'));
      pageEls.forEach((p) => {
        const c = p.querySelector('.content');
        if (!c || !c.childElementCount) p.remove();
      });

      const finalPages = pages.querySelectorAll('.page');
      finalPages.forEach((p, i) => {
        const num = p.querySelector('.footer .r');
        if (num) num.textContent = String(i + 1);
      });
    })();
  </script>
</body>
</html>
